# Ground-up audit coverage — 8 November 2026

Baseline `bcaf3d38ef03dca3b1607e0cb36c09ee3ea8f2b2`; repository `murphys-logs/tv-autocal`, default branch `main`. This inventory was made before conclusions and reconciled against `git ls-tree -r` afterwards. **108/108 baseline tracked files read in full by the Codex team**, without representative sampling. Reviewer labels identify internal OpenAI roles, not different vendors. Full-file reads, claim analysis and execution are distinct methods. The generated index does not itself establish correctness.

Read with the [audit verdict/findings](2026-11-08-ground-up-audit.md) and [claim/source register](../claim-notes.md#2026-11-08-ground-up-claim-register). Every baseline link below is pinned; line counts/digests deliberately describe the starting tree, not edited documents. SHA-256 can be reproduced from `git show <baseline>:<path>` bytes without running any application.

## Claim occurrence map

This records **every explicit C-ID occurrence** in all baseline files, including repeated claim notes, fixtures and archived reports. The claim register quotes each exact baseline proposition and evaluates it. Ranges such as C-001–C-128 are prose references, not 128 literal IDs at one line. Additional technical propositions without C-IDs are covered by A-R/A-S groups and the contract index below. A label occurrence is a locator, not independent support.

## Tests and implementation contract occurrences

All **287 test definitions**, and all **102 docstrings and 134 source comments** across the 34 baseline Python files, are indexed below. Each test body/assertion was read; its named contract is assessed with the owning A-S group. The additional **567 AST entries** are a filtered index, not a census of every definition: traversal follows module and class bodies, includes non-underscore classes/functions/methods and the `__init__`, `__call__`, `__enter__`, `__exit__` methods of public classes, and does not descend into function or control-flow bodies. Underscore-private classes and their members are excluded. Exact source propositions remain at pinned links rather than being paraphrased into new guarantees. Constants, fixtures, commands and bounds in the intervening source are included in the full-file read and A-S groups. No statistical coverage percentage is claimed.

The runner supplies hardware provenance.; [226](https://github.com/murphys-logs/tv-autocal/blob/bcaf3d38ef03dca3b1607e0cb36c09ee3ea8f2b2/tv_autocal_tools/meter.py#L226) A signal handler must not acquire the readers' lock or write logs.; [244](https://github.com/murphys-logs/tv-autocal/blob/bcaf3d38ef03dca3b1607e0cb36c09ee3ea8f2b2/tv_autocal_tools/meter.py#L244) The caller owns the capture lock (or all readers have joined).; [278](https://github.com/murphys-logs/tv-autocal/blob/bcaf3d38ef03dca3b1607e0cb36c09ee3ea8f2b2/tv_autocal_tools/meter.py#L278) With no process attached, even partially started workers simply exit.; [284](https://github.com/murphys-logs/tv-autocal/blob/bcaf3d38ef03dca3b1607e0cb36c09ee3ea8f2b2/tv_autocal_tools/meter.py#L284) A thread that never started has nothing to drain.; [364](https://github.com/murphys-logs/tv-autocal/blob/bcaf3d38ef03dca3b1607e0cb36c09ee3ea8f2b2/tv_autocal_tools/meter.py#L364) Defensive fallback for an explicitly raised KeyboardInterrupt.; [365](https://github.com/murphys-logs/tv-autocal/blob/bcaf3d38ef03dca3b1607e0cb36c09ee3ea8f2b2/tv_autocal_tools/meter.py#L365) Ordinary SIGINT uses the non-raising handler throughout this call.; [448](https://github.com/murphys-logs/tv-autocal/blob/bcaf3d38ef03dca3b1607e0cb36c09ee3ea8f2b2/tv_autocal_tools/meter.py#L448) Install failure is pre-launch. Keep the non-raising handler installed; [449](https://github.com/murphys-logs/tv-autocal/blob/bcaf3d38ef03dca3b1607e0cb36c09ee3ea8f2b2/tv_autocal_tools/meter.py#L449) until supervision, reader joins and pipe cleanup have actually completed.; [470](https://github.com/murphys-logs/tv-autocal/blob/bcaf3d38ef03dca3b1607e0cb36c09ee3ea8f2b2/tv_autocal_tools/meter.py#L470) A signal can arrive while the result is being built or the previous; [471](https://github.com/murphys-logs/tv-autocal/blob/bcaf3d38ef03dca3b1607e0cb36c09ee3ea8f2b2/tv_autocal_tools/meter.py#L471) handler is being restored. The child/readers are already finished,; [472](https://github.com/murphys-logs/tv-autocal/blob/bcaf3d38ef03dca3b1607e0cb36c09ee3ea8f2b2/tv_autocal_tools/meter.py#L472) but that late stop must still reject this attempt's returned result.; [481](https://github.com/murphys-logs/tv-autocal/blob/bcaf3d38ef03dca3b1607e0cb36c09ee3ea8f2b2/tv_autocal_tools/meter.py#L481) An unexpected post-launch failure is not reported as successful child; [482](https://github.com/murphys-logs/tv-autocal/blob/bcaf3d38ef03dca3b1607e0cb36c09ee3ea8f2b2/tv_autocal_tools/meter.py#L482) closure. Console/OS termination and arbitrary interpreter failures are; [483](https://github.com/murphys-logs/tv-autocal/blob/bcaf3d38ef03dca3b1607e0cb36c09ee3ea8f2b2/tv_autocal_tools/meter.py#L483) outside this bounded SIGINT policy; no termination/retry is attempted.

Reviewed API collections for PR1–38, relevant full review bodies and PR15/16/17 timelines. Q015's distinction between missing/late final-head reviews and a bot status summary remains justified. PR9's review is after merge; the early PR6/7/10 records return bot summaries without authored checklist reviews. PR13's returned authored review covers an earlier head. PR2 is more nuanced: its same head was later reviewed through PR4 before main, so do not state simply “no review.” PR15 `/reviews` is empty, but [issue comment 6415296243](https://github.com/murphys-logs/tv-autocal/pull/15#issuecomment-6415296243) contains an owner-relayed final-head Codex re-review of `fcd5570ac587d32f9bac2bf128a92d2171c21abb`; it is not missing merely because the API review array is empty.

PR16 initial Claude review [5651797994](https://github.com/murphys-logs/tv-autocal/pull/16#pullrequestreview-5651797994) covers `a93961ff99f90d692f0ab1a7c60ae0e900ec47ae`. All agent-authored posts use shared `murphys-logs`; vendor identities are self-attributions/owner relays, not cryptographic attestation. Current GitHub author-approval restriction supports COMMENT reviews with explicit recommendation. The audit requires two real new vendor reviews; this internal Codex checking, inherited review statements and silence cannot count.

PR17 head`51685ecfdb6f67fe6963eef8c337255986481c13` closed unmerged after review rejected purported lossless compression, altered owner quotation, dropped safety constraints and unsupported technical promotion. ## Audit additions and verification

New `tests/audit_ground_up.py` (118 lines) was fully read by the author and a second internal Codex reviewer before execution. It contains an inert console reproduction and independent arithmetic; it is intentionally outside unittest discovery. New report/coverage and appended claim analysis were internally checked for cross-links, ID coverage, source limits and archive preservation. The first-head source-docstring edit was reverted after Claude B1; all 34 manifest entries and all baseline application/test source bytes match again. Full-file reading did not prevent the missed upstream error interaction (A-007); the first audit overestimated C-056. The PR description records exact commands/head and review status. No claim of external independence follows.
